# Agents · A · part 13

944 entries, agentskills-skill-reviewer to ai-dev-loop-architect. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **agentskills-skill-reviewer**: Reviews Agent Skills against agentskills.io best practices for skill creation. Use when auditing a SKILL.md for expertise grounding, context efficiency, control calibration, and effective instruction patterns (templates, checklists, validation loops, plan-validate-execute) as defined by agentskills.io/skill-creation/best-practices. ([kayaman/skills](https://github.com/kayaman/skills/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/agentskills-skill-reviewer.agent.md) · ★3)
- **agentskills-specialist**: Use this agent when working with the AgentSkills skill framework — creating new skills, improving existing skills, reviewing skill definitions, ensuring cross-runtime compatibility, or auditing agent-skills:* skills for gaps. ([cyberuni/universal-plugin](https://github.com/cyberuni/universal-plugin/blob/HEAD/packages/universal-plugin/agents/agentskills-specialist.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)

- **agentspec:brainstorm-agent**: Collaborative exploration specialist for clarifying intent and approach (Phase 0). Use PROACTIVELY when users have raw ideas, vague requirements, or need to explore approaches. &lt;example&gt; Context: User has a raw idea without clear requirements user: "I want to build an automated data processing pipeline" assistant: "I'll use the brainstorm-agent to explore this idea and clarify requirements." &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: User needs to compare approaches user: "Should I use Lambda or Cloud Run for this?" assistant: "Let me invoke the brainstorm-agent to explore both approaches with trade-offs.… ([Arthur1511/agentspec-copilot](https://github.com/Arthur1511/agentspec-copilot/blob/HEAD/plugin-copilot/agents/workflow-brainstorm.agent.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **agentspec:build-agent**: Implementation executor with agent delegation (Phase 3). Use PROACTIVELY when design is complete and implementation is needed. &lt;example&gt; Context: User has a DESIGN document ready user: "Build the feature from DESIGN_AUTH_SYSTEM.md" assistant: "I'll use the build-agent to execute the implementation." &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: User wants to implement a designed feature user: "Implement the user authentication system" assistant: "Let me invoke the build-agent to build from the design." &lt;/example&gt; ([Arthur1511/agentspec-copilot](https://github.com/Arthur1511/agentspec-copilot/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/workflow-build.agent.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **agentspec:define-agent**: Requirements extraction and validation specialist (Phase 1). Use PROACTIVELY when users have requirements to capture or need to structure project scope. &lt;example&gt; Context: User has a brainstorm document ready user: "Define requirements from BRAINSTORM_AUTH_SYSTEM.md" assistant: "I'll use the define-agent to extract and validate requirements." &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: User has raw requirements user: "I need to capture requirements for the new auth system" assistant: "Let me invoke the define-agent to structure these requirements." &lt;/example&gt; ([Arthur1511/agentspec-copilot](https://github.com/Arthur1511/agentspec-copilot/blob/HEAD/plugin-copilot/agents/workflow-define.agent.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **agentspec:design-agent**: Architecture and technical specification specialist (Phase 2). Use PROACTIVELY when requirements are defined and technical design is needed. &lt;example&gt; Context: User has a DEFINE document ready user: "Design the architecture for DEFINE_AUTH_SYSTEM.md" assistant: "I'll use the design-agent to create the technical architecture." &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: User needs to plan implementation user: "How should we structure this feature?" assistant: "Let me invoke the design-agent to create a comprehensive design." &lt;/example&gt; ([Arthur1511/agentspec-copilot](https://github.com/Arthur1511/agentspec-copilot/blob/HEAD/plugin-copilot/agents/workflow-design.agent.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **agentspec:iterate-agent**: Cross-phase document updater with cascade awareness (All Phases). Use PROACTIVELY when requirements change mid-stream or documents need updating. &lt;example&gt; Context: Requirements changed after design started user: "Update DEFINE to add PDF support" assistant: "I'll use the iterate-agent to update with cascade awareness." &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: Design needs modification during build user: "Change the architecture to use Redis instead" assistant: "Let me invoke the iterate-agent to update DESIGN and check cascades." &lt;/example&gt; ([Arthur1511/agentspec-copilot](https://github.com/Arthur1511/agentspec-copilot/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/workflow-iterate.agent.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **agentspec:ship-agent**: Feature archival and lessons learned specialist (Phase 4). Use PROACTIVELY when build is complete and feature is ready to archive. &lt;example&gt; Context: Build is complete, ready to archive user: "Ship the user authentication feature" assistant: "I'll use the ship-agent to archive and capture lessons learned." &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: Feature needs to be documented as complete user: "Archive the completed auth feature" assistant: "Let me invoke the ship-agent to finalize and document." &lt;/example&gt; ([Arthur1511/agentspec-copilot](https://github.com/Arthur1511/agentspec-copilot/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/workflow-ship.agent.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)

Use when Iris delegates a layer after the wireframe is approved, or when a single frontend layer has to be written red-first against an approved wireframe. It writes one layer and stops; it does not draw or approve the wireframe, plan the build, open or merge pull requests, review its own work, or touch backend or data code. ([ahrenatechnology/ahrena-framework](https://github.com/ahrenatechnology/ahrena-framework/blob/HEAD/engineering/frontend/agents/aglaia.md) · ★1)

([zebbern/termstack](https://github.com/zebbern/termstack/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/agloop.agent.md) · ★4)
- **agnes-builder**: Disciplined Agnes feature implementer. Use when adding a data-source connector, REST API endpoint, web page, repository (method), or schema migration. Enforces the non-negotiables (TDD-first, DuckDB↔Postgres parity in the same change, migration-ladder sync, CHANGELOG, vendor-agnostic, scope discipline) and routes to the agnes-conventions playbooks. Writes code — it does not review (use /agnes-review for that). ([evviku/agnes-the-ai-analyst](https://github.com/evviku/agnes-the-ai-analyst/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agnes-builder.md))
- **agnes-decomposer**: Splits an implementation plan into INDEPENDENT tasks for parallel build, respecting the CONTRIBUTING.md sync-map coupling rules (parity siblings, migration steps, and merge-magnet files stay coupled). Read-only — emits a task graph, writes no code. Used by /agnes-build. ([evviku/agnes-the-ai-analyst](https://github.com/evviku/agnes-the-ai-analyst/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agnes-decomposer.md))
- **agnes-e2e-tester**: Use when verifying Agnes end-to-end — running/triaging the E2E suites (web chat, Slack, E2B, MCP), smoke-testing a feature before release, or walking the manual acceptance checklist against a live instance. Knows the env-var gates, the docker-compose harness, cost guardrails, and the per-surface verification checklists. Verifies and reports — it does not implement features (use agnes-builder for that). ([evviku/agnes-the-ai-analyst](https://github.com/evviku/agnes-the-ai-analyst/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agnes-e2e-tester.md))
- **agnes-integrator**: Integrates the build team's parallel worktree results into one coherent change — applies tasks in dependency order, serializes the single migration task last, folds CHANGELOG/CLAUDE.md bullets in one pass, and stops on any unresolvable conflict. Used by /agnes-build. ([evviku/agnes-the-ai-analyst](https://github.com/evviku/agnes-the-ai-analyst/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agnes-integrator.md))
- **agnes-releaser**: Use before merging a PR (phase 1 — prepare release-cut commit) and after merge (phase 2 — tag + GitHub Release). Invoked explicitly by the user; never auto-fires. Never merges the PR. ([evviku/agnes-the-ai-analyst](https://github.com/evviku/agnes-the-ai-analyst/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agnes-releaser.md))
- **agnes-review-consolidator**: Final consolidator for the agnes-review team. Merges per-reviewer findings into one advisory report — dedup, severity escalation on multi-reviewer overlap, ≤15 findings, file:line + severity each. ([evviku/agnes-the-ai-analyst](https://github.com/evviku/agnes-the-ai-analyst/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agnes-review-consolidator.md))
- **agnes-reviewer-architecture**: Use when a PR diff touches src/orchestrator.py, src/db.py, connectors/*/extractor.py, or adds a schema migration. ([evviku/agnes-the-ai-analyst](https://github.com/evviku/agnes-the-ai-analyst/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agnes-reviewer-architecture.md))
- **agnes-reviewer-parity**: Use when a PR diff touches src/repositories/*, src/db.py, migrations/, or tests/db_pg/. Verifies DuckDB↔Postgres parity — matching _pg.py sibling, factory dispatch entry, contract test, and the Alembic↔db.py migration ladder — and flags backend-split drift the existing guards cannot see. ([evviku/agnes-the-ai-analyst](https://github.com/evviku/agnes-the-ai-analyst/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agnes-reviewer-parity.md))
- **agnes-reviewer-rbac**: Use when a PR diff touches app/api/, app/auth/, or app/resource_types.py. Checks that new endpoints have correct gates (require_admin or require_resource_access) and that new ResourceType values are registered with a ResourceTypeSpec. ([evviku/agnes-the-ai-analyst](https://github.com/evviku/agnes-the-ai-analyst/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agnes-reviewer-rbac.md))
- **agnes-reviewer-rules**: Use at the end of PR work to enforce Agnes conventions — CHANGELOG bullet (smart, not blind), vendor-agnostic content, no AI attribution, issue economy, clean commits. Fast, runs on every PR. ([evviku/agnes-the-ai-analyst](https://github.com/evviku/agnes-the-ai-analyst/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agnes-reviewer-rules.md))

- **Agno Agent Tester**: Use when testing an implemented Agno agent against its acceptance criteria. - **Agno Design Reviewer**: Use when reviewing an Agno agent design plan produced by the Agno Agent Designer. Write report to adhd_project_files/agno-agent-designs/. ([pedrogrande/adf](https://github.com/pedrogrande/adf/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/agno-design-reviewer.agent.md))

- **agnt_brisken-mail-reader**: READ-ONLY reader of Brisken's Microsoft 365 mail over Graph, limited to exactly three mailboxes (dirk.neumann@, matthias.silva@, [email removed]). Use when a question can only be answered from Brisken mail - where a receipt or invoice arrived, whether a vendor or sender mailed in a window, which attachments a message carries, whether a mailbox is reachable at all - and for "has this lead been contacted" via the outreach truth tool. Proves its search can see before it reports anything as absent. Never sends, drafts, moves, flags or deletes. Answers FOUND, NOT FOUND or BLIND. ([011matthias/agentic-ops1.01](https://github.com/011matthias/agentic-ops1.01/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agnt_brisken-mail-reader.md))
- **agnt_bug-fixer**: Analyzes test failures and implements fixes. Use proactively when tests fail, after deployment issues, or when errors are reported. Minimal changes principle with verification loop. Diagnoses root causes, implements fixes, and re-runs tests to verify. ([011matthias/agentic-ops1.01](https://github.com/011matthias/agentic-ops1.01/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agnt_bug-fixer.md))
- **agnt_build-orchestrator**: Main coordinator for end-to-end automation building. Orchestrates Plan → Implement → Test → Document → Deploy → Verify phases with specialized agents. Manages agent handoffs, session state, and approval gates. Use for complete automation development lifecycle. ([011matthias/agentic-ops1.01](https://github.com/011matthias/agentic-ops1.01/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agnt_build-orchestrator.md))
- **agnt_comms-critic**: Audits a /comd_draft output against the comms feedback memories and the comms-log thread before the user sees it. Returns OK or a numbered fix list. Use ONLY after a client message draft has been written and before presenting it to the user. Does not modify files. ([011matthias/agentic-ops1.01](https://github.com/011matthias/agentic-ops1.01/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agnt_comms-critic.md))
- **agnt_deployer**: Deploys automations to Railway with test gates. Use when ready to ship a client automation, after code changes, or for production deployment. Runs tests first and only deploys if all pass. ([011matthias/agentic-ops1.01](https://github.com/011matthias/agentic-ops1.01/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agnt_deployer.md))
- **agnt_done-verifier**: Independently verifies "done / shipped / deployed / fixed" claims before they reach the user. Use when the main loop is about to declare a web deploy, an SPA or app change, an HTML deliverable, or an external state change complete. Drives single-page apps in a cold headless browser and asserts the changed value renders (a fetch only sees the shell), validates HTML, checks gh/CLI state, and keeps "my probe could not see" apart from "the change did not land". Returns VERIFIED or a numbered failure list. Does not modify files. ([011matthias/agentic-ops1.01](https://github.com/011matthias/agentic-ops1.01/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agnt_done-verifier.md))
- **agnt_implementation-agent**: Generates production-ready automation code from specifications. Use proactively when implementing new automations from specs or updating existing implementations. Creates automation classes, tests, router integrations, and configuration updates following workspace patterns. ([011matthias/agentic-ops1.01](https://github.com/011matthias/agentic-ops1.01/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agnt_implementation-agent.md))
- **agnt_intent-reviewer**: Audits a proposed plan or spec against the originating user input before the plan is executed. Catches over-literal / intent-misalignment / strategic-gap patterns at planning time. Returns OK or a numbered findings list. Use BEFORE the main loop spends effort executing a plan, especially after spec creation or whenever a proposed direction was inferred from exploratory or mixed user input. Does not modify files; does not rewrite the plan. ([011matthias/agentic-ops1.01](https://github.com/011matthias/agentic-ops1.01/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agnt_intent-reviewer.md))
- **agnt_make-builder**: Make.com scenario builder specialist. Use when implementing or updating a Make.com spec — generates blueprint JSON, runs pre-build gates (ops estimation, UI-impossibility check, connection enumeration), deploys via MCP with REST fallback, and emits a parseable build report. Does not run executions (testing-agent handles that) and does not touch n8n / Trigger.dev / Python code. ([011matthias/agentic-ops1.01](https://github.com/011matthias/agentic-ops1.01/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agnt_make-builder.md))
- **agnt_n8n-builder**: n8n workflow builder specialist. Use when implementing or updating an n8n spec — generates workflow JSON, runs pre-build gates (nodeType format, typeVersion compatibility, Code-node sandbox restrictions, expression-syntax integrity, sheet-op ordering, credential enumeration), deploys via the per-client n8n MCP server with iterative partial-update pattern, and emits a parseable build report. Does not run executions (testing-agent handles that), does not activate workflows in production (deployer agent does that), and does not touch Make.com / Trigger.dev / Python code. ([011matthias/agentic-ops1.01](https://github.com/011matthias/agentic-ops1.01/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agnt_n8n-builder.md))
- **agnt_proposal-research**: Produces the populated `research:` block + requirement coverage matrix for a new proposal by running concurrent research fan-out over an Upwork job posting (or equivalent). Returns a synthesis report OR a BLOCKED list if research inputs are insufficient. Use during Step 2 (Research Gate) of /comd_new-proposal, before any cover letter / video script / HTML site is written. Does not write the proposal deliverables themselves. ([011matthias/agentic-ops1.01](https://github.com/011matthias/agentic-ops1.01/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agnt_proposal-research.md))
- **agnt_sourced-researcher**: Web research for market, competitor, pricing, channel and GTM questions where every number and load-bearing claim must carry a source, for UnpauseAI growth work and client strategy. Use when delegating a research question whose answer feeds a decision or a deliverable, including parallel fan-outs of several such questions. Returns a direct answer, a sourced findings table and an explicit gap list; anything it cannot source is TBD, never estimated silently. Not for Upwork posting research (agnt_proposal-research), repo or codebase search (Explore), or Brisken mail (agnt_brisken-mail-reader). ([011matthias/agentic-ops1.01](https://github.com/011matthias/agentic-ops1.01/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agnt_sourced-researcher.md))
- **agnt_testing-agent**: Validates automation implementations against specifications and manages testing status. Use proactively after automation code changes to run tests, execute dry-runs, verify acceptance criteria, update automation-status.yaml, and verify production health. This file is everything you need. Follow it exactly. ([useago/ago-sdk](https://github.com/useago/ago-sdk/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ago-angular.md) · ★5)
- **ago-react**: You are integrating the AGO SDK (`@useago/sdk`) into a React app. AGO is a chat agent that can answer questions, run functions in the user's browser, and navigate your app's routes. This file is everything you need. Follow it exactly. ([useago/ago-sdk](https://github.com/useago/ago-sdk/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ago-react.md) · ★5)
- **ago-vanilla**: You are integrating the AGO SDK (`@useago/sdk`) into a plain JS/TS app (no React, Vue, or Angular), a Node service, or a web component. AGO is a chat agent that can answer questions, run functions in the user's browser, and navigate your app. The core `AgoClient` documented here is what every framework binding wraps. This file is everything you need. Follow it exactly. ([useago/ago-sdk](https://github.com/useago/ago-sdk/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ago-vanilla.md) · ★5)
- **ago-vue**: You are integrating the AGO SDK (`@useago/sdk`) into a Vue 3 app. AGO is a chat agent that can answer questions, run functions in the user's browser, and navigate your app's routes. - **ago-widget**: You are adding the AGO chat widget to a website with a `&lt;script&gt;` tag. No build step and no npm install. This is the right approach for a marketing site, a CMS (WordPress, Webflow), or any server-rendered page where you don't control a JS bundle. For a React/Vue/Angular app, use the framework binding instead. This file is everything you need. Follow it exactly. ([useago/ago-sdk](https://github.com/useago/ago-sdk/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ago-widget.md) · ★5)
- **agol**: Advisor — Global continuous-reasoning advisor. Invoke when a session needs broad fluid synthesis across phases, projects, or stacks — not a challenger (Janus holds that role), not a planner. Named for Henrietta Swan Leavitt's 1908 observation on variable stars: "Apparently no sharp dividing line can be drawn between true Algol stars and those whose variations are continuous." Technical mapping: an advisor that holds reasoning across phase boundaries without forcing a verdict shape. Runs on Fable. ([hruzam/ia-sync](https://github.com/hruzam/ia-sync/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/agol.md))

- **agrarrecht-researcher**: Du bist die **Recherche-Stufe** in der Researcher → Drafter → Reviewer-Pipeline. - **agrarrecht-reviewer**: Du bist die **Qualitäts- und Risikostufe** vor Auslieferung an den Mandantenanwalt oder die zuständige Behörde / das Landwirtschaftsgericht. - **AGS-20261120-g7hxr0-dynamic-boost-on-curiosity-with-driver-620-70-06-why-nvidia**: - The display, 620.70.06 and `n1x-tablet-mode.service` (started at boot by itself) work. nvidia-powerd still quit. - Traced the failure into the open driver: NVPCF 3.x reads get a quarter of the caller's buffer, too small for NPCF's 2,176-byte init table. Wrote `nix/patches/nvidia-open-nvpcf3x-read-size.patch`, built it from neusis for the booted kernel (anvesana overridden to this checkout, neusi ([leoank/anvesana](https://github.com/leoank/anvesana/blob/HEAD/notes/agents/AGS-20261120-g7hxr0-dynamic-boost-on-curiosity-with-driver-620-70-06-why-nvidia.md))

([leoank/anvesana](https://github.com/leoank/anvesana/blob/HEAD/notes/agents/AGS-20261120-t10mvw-test-whether-msi-center-s-top-mode-carries-the-gpu-cap-into.md))

- **agt-01-seo-visibility**: Владеет всей SEO целиком: коммерческая стратегия (ключевые слова, интент, кластеры, roadmap) и техническая SEO (schema.org, hreflang, sitemap, robots, Google Business Profile, ссылочный профиль). Заменяет SEO_STRATEGIST + SEO_TECH. Use when planning SEO priorities or diagnosing/fixing technical SEO issues. ([780443k4-cmd/skyline-engineering](https://github.com/780443k4-cmd/skyline-engineering/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agt-01-seo-visibility.md))
- **agt-02-content-proof**: Ведёт контент от брифа до финального текста: структура, копирайтинг, факт-чек, anti-AI-slop, карусели и визуальные форматы. Заменяет CONTENT_ARCHITECT + CONTENT_WRITER (и мандат так и не созданного CREATIVE_DIRECTOR). Use when producing or editing any content piece for the calendar. ([780443k4-cmd/skyline-engineering](https://github.com/780443k4-cmd/skyline-engineering/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agt-02-content-proof.md))
- **agt-03-social-ops**: Стратегия соцсетей, платформенная адаптация и аналитика по Instagram/Facebook/TikTok. Заменяет SOCIAL_STRATEGIST + SOCIAL_CONTENT + SOCIAL_ANALYST. Use when planning, adapting, publishing to, or analyzing performance on Instagram, Facebook or TikTok. ([780443k4-cmd/skyline-engineering](https://github.com/780443k4-cmd/skyline-engineering/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agt-03-social-ops.md))
- **agt-04-knowledge-intel**: Держит базу знаний, 4 стратегических документа и их производные, конкурентную разведку и квалификацию лидов в актуальном состоянии. Заменяет KNOWLEDGE_MANAGER + COMPETITOR_ANALYST + LEAD_ANALYST. Use when a fact needs sourcing/verification, or competitor/lead data needs updating. ([780443k4-cmd/skyline-engineering](https://github.com/780443k4-cmd/skyline-engineering/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agt-04-knowledge-intel.md))
- **agt-05-release-gate**: Финальный контроль качества и решение APPROVE/REJECT/REVISE перед любой публикацией, с обязательной записью в audit trail. Заменяет QUALITY_CONTROLLER + FINAL_GATEKEEPER. Use as the last step before anything goes live — content, site changes, or external actions. ([780443k4-cmd/skyline-engineering](https://github.com/780443k4-cmd/skyline-engineering/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agt-05-release-gate.md))
- **agt-architecture**: Technical design agent for this repository. Transforms approved requirements.md into docs/specs/&lt;feature-slug&gt;/design.md, mapping layers, contracts, persistence, messaging, compatibility, idempotency, observability, rollout and rollback. Writes only under docs/specs/. Never edits src/ and never redefines product rules silently. ([beardjs/ai-backend-kit](https://github.com/beardjs/ai-backend-kit/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/agt-architecture.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **agt-architecture-analyst**: Consolidates docs/architecture/profile.md and patterns.md into a single narrative docs/architecture/analysis.md. Dispatches probe/miner when those sources are missing (unless SKIPPED_LAYERED_KIT). Does not edit application source or kit rules. Use after Path D profile/patterns or when the user asks for one complete architecture analysis markdown. ([beardjs/ai-backend-kit](https://github.com/beardjs/ai-backend-kit/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/agt-architecture-analyst.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **agt-architecture-probe**: Architecture-agnostic probe. Inventories a repository as-is, classifies style without dogma (layered, hexagonal, MVC, hybrid, etc.), maps observed boundaries and dependency directions, and writes docs/architecture/profile.md. Does not edit application source or Cursor rules. Coexists with agt-architecture (SDD design) and agt-architecture-review (layered audit). ([beardjs/ai-backend-kit](https://github.com/beardjs/ai-backend-kit/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/agt-architecture-probe.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **agt-architecture-review**: Architecture audit for Node.js/TypeScript layered backends (Domain / Application / Infraestructure / Configuration). Validates layer separation, contracts, factories, Mongo adapters and improper coupling. ([beardjs/ai-backend-kit](https://github.com/beardjs/ai-backend-kit/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/agt-architecture-review.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **agt-code-quality**: Audits semantic naming, REST conventions, and light layer adherence in layered backend services. Use for PR review, pre-merge quality checks, or when the user asks for code quality validation (not full architecture audit). ([beardjs/ai-backend-kit](https://github.com/beardjs/ai-backend-kit/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/agt-code-quality.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **agt-code-review**: Read-only spec-aware code review agent for this repository. Compares requirements ↔ design ↔ tasks ↔ implementation ↔ tests, classifies findings as BLOCKING_FUNCTIONAL / BLOCKING_ARCHITECTURE / BLOCKING_SECURITY / BLOCKING_CONTRACT / NON_BLOCKING_IMPROVEMENT / STYLE / QUESTION, and routes blocking findings back to the correct owner. Never edits code. ([beardjs/ai-backend-kit](https://github.com/beardjs/ai-backend-kit/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/agt-code-review.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **agt-dev-backend**: Backend Node.js/TypeScript development in this repo, following AGENTS.md and docs/architecture-and-layers.md. Works with Express, MongoDB, factories, contracts, tests and small/focused changes. ([beardjs/ai-backend-kit](https://github.com/beardjs/ai-backend-kit/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/agt-dev-backend.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **agt-dev-commit**: Specialized subagent that analyzes code changes, groups them by context, and suggests Conventional Commits following best practices. Uses GitHub CLI for PR creation. ([whitebeardit/.cursor](https://github.com/whitebeardit/.cursor/blob/HEAD/agents/agt-dev-commit.md) · ★12)

- **agt-dev-dotnet-logging**: Specialized subagent that analyzes code changes in the current context and adds appropriate logging statements using correlation-id tracking patterns. Defines product outcomes, actors, business rules, flows, acceptance criteria, non-functional expectations, risks, assumptions, metrics, and out-of-scope. Use before architecture, QA planning, or implementation. Never implements code.
